    @pytest.mark.contract
    def test_wording_hit_refuses_the_write(tmp_path, capsys):
        # A registry whose rendered line would carry blocking wording: the sandbox owner
        # gains a lowercase blocking heading, an agent handler anchors on it.
        registry = REGISTRY_TEXT.replace(
            "| h-run-determination | engine | determine | non-blocking | 10 | "
            "scripts/python/run-determination.py |",
            "| h-run-determination | engine | determine | non-blocking | 10 | "
            "scripts/python/run-determination.py |\n"
            "| h-stop-and-confirm | agent | stop and confirm the draft | non-blocking "
            "| 25 | shared/workflow/feedback-step.md |")
        # Anchor at the table header separator: inserting mid-row (substring inside a
        # row carrying a 覆盖说明 cell) would corrupt the row's cells.
        registry = registry.replace(
            "| handler_id | selector | scope | 覆盖说明 |\n"
            "|------------|----------|-------|----------|",
            "| handler_id | selector | scope | 覆盖说明 |\n"
            "|------------|----------|-------|----------|\n"
            "| h-stop-and-confirm | @point:wrap-up | all | wording-gate drill row |")
        ws = CE._build_workspace(tmp_path, registry)
        owner = ws / "shared" / "workflow" / "feedback-step.md"
        owner.write_text(
            owner.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
            + "\n## stop and confirm the draft\n\nDrill heading.\n",
            encoding="utf-8")
        rc = probe_utils.main(["--action", "compile-embeds",
                               "--workspace-root", str(ws)])
        assert rc == 2, "generated wording hitting BLOCKING_PATTERNS must refuse (exit 2)"
        err = capsys.readouterr().err
>       assert "wording gate" in err, f"the refusal must name the wording gate: {err}"
E       AssertionError: the refusal must name the wording gate: error: compile-embeds refused:
E         - handler 'h-closeout': behavior_owner unreachable: shared/workflow/artifact-commit-step.md
E         
E       assert 'wording gate' in "error: compile-embeds refused:\n- handler 'h-closeout': behavior_owner unreachable: shared/workflow/artifact-commit-step.md\n"

    def test_c5_non_wiring_files_keep_their_frozen_hashes():
        manifest = _hash_manifest()
        templates = [rel for rel in manifest if rel.startswith("templates/commands/")]
        skills = [rel for rel in manifest if rel.startswith("skills/")]
        assert len(manifest) == 74, "hash manifest must cover 74 sources, found %d" % len(manifest)
        assert len(templates) == 25, "hash manifest must cover 25 templates, found %d" % len(templates)
        assert len(skills) == 49, "hash manifest must cover 49 skills, found %d" % len(skills)
        assert not any(rel.endswith(tuple(WIRING_TEMPLATES)) for rel in templates), (
            "the manifest is the NON-wiring complement; it still lists a wiring template"
        )
        assert not any(rel in WIRING_SKILLS for rel in skills), (
            "the manifest is the NON-wiring complement; it still lists a wiring skill"
        )
    
        # 2026-10-30 (Feature 078): the one renamed skill source is resolved through
        # the register above, then re-asserted on content at its new path — the
        # manifest entry itself stays untouched as 069a's dated record.
        retired = _retired_skill_register(manifest)
    
        drifted: list[str] = []
        missing: list[str] = []
        for rel, expected in sorted(manifest.items()):
            if rel in retired:
                continue
            path = REPO_ROOT / rel
            if not path.is_file():
                missing.append(rel)
                continue
            if _sha256(path) != expected:
                if rel in ALLOWED_POST_BASELINE_CHANGES:
                    continue
                drifted.append(rel)
        assert not missing, (
            "non-wiring source disappeared (C-5, zero new commands/skills): %r" % missing
        )
>       assert not drifted, (
            "non-wiring source changed (C-5: the slice may only touch W1-W4 and the "
            "feedback-step pointer). drifted: %r" % drifted
        )
E       AssertionError: non-wiring source changed (C-5: the slice may only touch W1-W4 and the feedback-step pointer). drifted: ['templates/commands/agents.md', 'templates/commands/constitution.md', 'templates/commands/feature.md']
E       assert not ['templates/commands/agents.md', 'templates/commands/constitution.md', 'templates/commands/feature.md']

    def test_c2_no_project_side_classification_store():
        offenders: list[str] = []
        for path in sorted(MEMORY_DIR.rglob("*")):
            if not path.is_file():
                continue
            rel = path.relative_to(REPO_ROOT).as_posix()
            if CLASS_FILE_NAME_RE.search(path.name):
                offenders.append("%s — classification-named file" % rel)
                continue
            try:
                text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
            except (UnicodeDecodeError, OSError):
                continue
            for number, line in enumerate(text.splitlines(), 1):
                if LEDGER_ROW_RE.match(line):
                    offenders.append("%s:%d — ledger-style unit/class row" % (rel, number))
                elif CLASS_ASSIGN_RE.search(line):
                    offenders.append("%s:%d — flow_class assignment" % (rel, number))
>       assert not offenders, (

    def test_c57_shipped_text_adds_no_fresh_neutrality_violation():
        violations: Dict[str, List[str]] = {}
        for root_name in ("templates", "shared", "skills", "agents"):
            for path in (FRAMEWORK_ROOT / root_name).rglob("*"):
                if not path.is_file() or path.suffix not in {".md", ".py", ".sh", ".yaml", ".yml", ".json"}:
                    continue
                rel = path.relative_to(FRAMEWORK_ROOT).as_posix()
                text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
                for pattern in (
                    re.compile(r"\b(?:req|requirement)\s+\d{3}\b", re.IGNORECASE),
                    re.compile(r"\bFeature\s+\d{3}\b"),
                    re.compile(r"\.specify/specs/\d{3}-"),
                    re.compile(r"\.specify/memory/features/\d{3}"),
                ):
                    if pattern.search(text):
                        violations.setdefault(rel, []).append(pattern.pattern)
        fresh = sorted(set(violations) - PRE_EXISTING_NEUTRALITY_DEBT)
>       assert not fresh, (
            "C-57: Feature 072 shipped text introduced fresh framework-runtime "
            "references:\n" + "\n".join(fresh)
        )
E       AssertionError: C-57: Feature 072 shipped text introduced fresh framework-runtime references:
E         shared/declarations/contract.yaml
E         shared/declarations/facts/concurrency-contract.yaml
E         shared/declarations/facts/goal-team-command-family.yaml
E         shared/declarations/facts/goal-team-handoff-threshold.yaml

    def test_canonical_engine_does_not_self_resolve_to_the_framework_repo(tmp_path: Path):
        """Run the CANONICAL engine from a workspace that is not this repo.
    
        Before the fix, resolve_root walked up looking for any ancestor containing
        `.specify/` — which the framework repo always does — so every invocation
        resolved here and wrote workspace state into the repo.

        (tmp_path / ".specify" / "memory").mkdir(parents=True)

        module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
        sys.modules[spec.name] = module
        spec.loader.exec_module(module)
    
        prev = Path.cwd()
        import os
        os.chdir(tmp_path)
        try:
            resolved = module.resolve_root(None)
        finally:
            os.chdir(prev)

________________ test_skills_use_installed_absolute_shared_form ________________
[gw162] linux -- Python 3.11.11 /usr/local/bin/python3

    def test_skills_use_installed_absolute_shared_form():
        """Sibling skills reference shared docs via the installed-absolute shared path.
    
        Scans every markdown file in a skill, not just SKILL.md: the references/ files ship
        into `.specify/skills/<name>/` too, and a bare `shared/<type>/` path there is just as
        unresolvable in a client project. Scanning only the top-level file left three of them
        undetected while the test was red for an unrelated reason.
        """
        import re
    
        offenders = []
        scanned = 0
        bare = re.compile(r"(?<![\w./])shared/(" + "|".join(SHARED_TYPES) + r")/")
        for f in _iter_files("skills/**/*.md"):
            scanned += 1
            text = f.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
            if bare.search(text):
                offenders.append(str(f.relative_to(ROOT)))
        assert scanned > 100, (
            f"only {scanned} skill markdown files scanned — the glob looks truncated, which "
            f"would let an empty offender list pass without proving anything"
        )
>       assert not offenders, f"skills must use .specify/shared/<type>/ form: {offenders}"
E       AssertionError: skills must use .specify/shared/<type>/ form: ['skills/internal/analyze-artifacts/references/detection-and-severity.md', 'skills/internal/review-feature-quality/SKILL.md', 'skills/internal/review-feature-quality/references/artifact-and-implementation-review.md', 'skills/internal/run-skills/SKILL.md', 'skills/internal/create-plan/SKILL.md', 'skills/internal/memory-recall/SKILL.md', 'skills/internal/code-review/SKILL.md', 'skills/internal/create-team/references/create-mode.md', 'skills/internal/create-team/references/conceptual-model.md', 'skills/internal/manage-history/SKILL.md', 'skills/internal/manage-feedback/SKILL.md', 'skills/internal/manage-feedback/references/path-a-digest.md', 'skills/internal/review-sdd-process/SKILL.md', 'skills/internal/memory-record/SKILL.md', 'skills/internal/implement-feature/SKILL.md', 'skills/external/create-docs/SKILL.md', 'skills/external/create-docs/references/diagram-obligation.md', 'skills/external/merge-skills/SKILL.md', 'skills/external/merge-skills/references/provenance-record.md', 'skills/external/sdd-loop-driver/templates/stage-team-preset.md', 'skills/external/sdd-loop-driver/references/dispatch-recipes.md', 'skills/external/sdd-loop-driver/references/stage-prompts.md', 'skills/external/sdd-loop-driver/references/nested-invocation.md', 'skills/external/humanize/SKILL.md', 'skills/external/humanize/references/report-shape.md', 'skills/external/humanize/references/run-mode.md', 'skills/external/git-utils/references/closeout-commands.md', 'skills/external/create-skills/SKILL.md', 'skills/external/create-skills/references/mechanism-policy-check.md', 'skills/external/sdd-house-keeping/references/maintenance-recipes.md', 'skills/external/coaching-skills/SKILL.md', 'skills/external/improve-skills/SKILL.md']

self = <json.decoder.JSONDecoder object at 0x7f0342bb8290>, s = '', idx = 0

    def raw_decode(self, s, idx=0):
        """Decode a JSON document from ``s`` (a ``str`` beginning with
        a JSON document) and return a 2-tuple of the Python
        representation and the index in ``s`` where the document ended.
    
        This can be used to decode a JSON document from a string that may
        have extraneous data at the end.

[gw147] linux -- Python 3.11.11 /usr/local/bin/python3

    def test_c11_no_dangling_guideline_pointer_on_either_instruction_surface():
        surfaces = (("template", TEMPLATE), ("live", LIVE))
        pointed: set[str] = set()
        for label, path in surfaces:
            names = set(GUIDELINE_POINTER_RE.findall(_text(path)))
            assert names, f"the {label} instruction surface carries no guideline pointer at all"
            pointed |= names
    
        # A pointer's whole job is to let a reader open the file, so the target that must exist
        # is the one the reader actually resolves: `.specify/shared/guidelines/<name>.md`.
        # Checking only the framework source leaves the downstream failure mode invisible —
        # proven by drill: with the runtime copy moved aside and the source intact, a
        # source-only version of this clause still passed. Both halves are asserted, because
        # the source is what regenerates the runtime copy.
        runtime_dir = ROOT / ".specify" / "shared" / "guidelines"
        dangling_runtime = sorted(n for n in pointed if not (runtime_dir / n).is_file())
        assert not dangling_runtime, (
            "instruction surfaces point at guidelines with no runtime copy under "
            ".specify/shared/guidelines/ — a reader following the pointer finds nothing, which "
            "is the delivery window FR-038 / SC-017 exists to close "
            f"(run scripts/python/sync-mirrors.py --write shared): {dangling_runtime}"
        )
        dangling_source = sorted(n for n in pointed if not (GUIDELINES / n).is_file())
        assert not dangling_source, (
            "instruction surfaces point at guidelines with no framework source under "
            f"shared/guidelines/ — the runtime copy cannot be regenerated: {dangling_source}"
        )
    
        # C-11(b): the remainder must not grow past the three structurally unpointed files.
        # Anti-vacuity: name the three and require them to exist, so an empty guidelines
        # directory cannot make the subset assertion pass on nothing.
        for name in UNPOINTED_BY_STRUCTURE:
            assert (GUIDELINES / name).is_file(), (
                f"C-11(b) names {name} as unpointed-by-structure but it is not in shared/guidelines/"
            )
        full = {p.name for p in GUIDELINES.glob("*.md")}
        remainder = full - pointed
        unexpected = sorted(remainder - set(UNPOINTED_BY_STRUCTURE))
>       assert not unexpected, (
            "a guideline lost its instruction-surface pointer, so it can no longer dangle "
            "loudly — coverage shrank silently. Either restore the pointer or amend "
            f"ambient-section.md C-11(b) with a recorded reason: {unexpected}"
        )
E       AssertionError: a guideline lost its instruction-surface pointer, so it can no longer dangle loudly — coverage shrank silently. Either restore the pointer or amend ambient-section.md C-11(b) with a recorded reason: ['reporting-obligations.md', 'self-explanatory.md']
E       assert not ['reporting-obligations.md', 'self-explanatory.md']

[gw53] linux -- Python 3.11.11 /usr/local/bin/python3

tmp_path = PosixPath('/tmp/pytest-of-agent/pytest-5787/popen-gw53/test_cli_subprocess_smoke_pins0')

    def test_cli_subprocess_smoke_pins_payload_and_exit_semantics(tmp_path: Path):
        """≥1 true CLI subprocess run stays in this file (FR-020).
    
        Pins the engine's CLI contract on a real interpreter subprocess — fixed
        9-key envelope, ok/errors shape, graded exit codes — and asserts the
        in-process runner used by every other test in this file produces the
        *same* payload and exit code for identical argv on a twin workspace. If a
        future engine change makes the two faces diverge, this pin fires.
        """
        ws_cli = tmp_path / "cli"
        ws_ipc = tmp_path / "ipc"
        for d in (ws_cli, ws_ipc):
            (d / ".specify" / "templates").mkdir(parents=True)
            (d / ".specify" / "templates" / "proactive-trigger-seed.json").write_bytes(
                SEED.read_bytes()
            )
            (d / ".specify" / "memory").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            subprocess.run(["git", "init", "-q", "."], cwd=d, check=True)
    
        # init: envelope shape + ok flag, pinned on the subprocess face.
        proc_cli = cli_run(["--action", "init"], ws_cli)
>       assert proc_cli.returncode == EXIT_OK, proc_cli.stderr

/usr/lib64/python3.11/pathlib.py:1044: FileNotFoundError
____ test_maturity_promotion_does_not_retroactively_rewrite_earlier_states _____
[gw68] linux -- Python 3.11.11 /usr/local/bin/python3

tmp_path = PosixPath('/tmp/pytest-of-agent/pytest-5787/popen-gw68/test_maturity_promotion_does_n0')

    @requires_generator
    def test_maturity_promotion_does_not_retroactively_rewrite_earlier_states(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
        """LC-10 — report-only-era items stay as recorded after an L1 -> L2 promotion.
    
        The property holds because the ledger is append-only (IL-1) and each event
        carries the maturity in force when it happened; a later promotion appends, it
        never mutates. This asserts that the generator honours that rather than
        re-deriving state from the team's *current* maturity.
        """
        (tmp_path / ".specify/teams/mat/runs").mkdir(parents=True)
        (tmp_path / ".specify/teams/mat/team.md").write_text(
            team_md("mat", pattern="continuous"), encoding="utf-8"
        )
        (tmp_path / ".specify/teams/mat/runs/20260710T080000Z-report.md").write_text(
            CONTRACT_REPORT.format(slug="mat", day="10", outcome="completed", gen=1, deliverable="产物"),
            encoding="utf-8",
        )
        ledger = tmp_path / ".specify/teams/mat/items.jsonl"

repo_root = PosixPath('/tmp/pytest-of-agent/pytest-5787/popen-gw162/test_global_scope_paths0')
scope = 'global', spec_dir = None, team_slug = None

    def resolve_scope(repo_root: Path, scope: str = "goal",
                      spec_dir: str | os.PathLike | None = None,
                      team_slug: str | None = None) -> Scope:
        """Resolve the two archive-layer roots. The path rule has exactly one owner: this function.
    
        `scope` is the closed archive-layer set `goal` / `teams` / `auto` (C-9). `goal` and
        `teams` always return BOTH roots in one `Scope` object, so a caller never needs two
        calls; `auto` is a read-only resolver that picks the single layer containing the
        requested slug (or the single non-empty layer when no slug is requested).
        """
        repo_root = Path(repo_root)
        goal_root = repo_root / ARCHIVE_DIRNAME
        team_root_path = repo_root / TEAM_DIRNAME
        if scope not in ARCHIVE_SCOPES:
>           raise ScopeError(
                f"scope {scope!r} is outside the closed set {' / '.join(ARCHIVE_SCOPES)}")
E           goal_utils_scope.ScopeError: scope 'global' is outside the closed set goal / teams / auto

tests/unit/test_goal_scope_engine.py:79: 
_ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ 

repo_root = PosixPath('/tmp/pytest-of-agent/pytest-5787/popen-gw156/test_auto_prefers_spec_when_on0')
scope = 'auto'
spec_dir = PosixPath('/tmp/pytest-of-agent/pytest-5787/popen-gw156/test_auto_prefers_spec_when_on0/.specify/specs/084-sample')
team_slug = None

    def resolve_scope(repo_root: Path, scope: str = "goal",
                      spec_dir: str | os.PathLike | None = None,
                      team_slug: str | None = None) -> Scope:
        """Resolve the two archive-layer roots. The path rule has exactly one owner: this function.
    
        `scope` is the closed archive-layer set `goal` / `teams` / `auto` (C-9). `goal` and
        `teams` always return BOTH roots in one `Scope` object, so a caller never needs two
        calls; `auto` is a read-only resolver that picks the single layer containing the
        requested slug (or the single non-empty layer when no slug is requested).
        """
        repo_root = Path(repo_root)
        goal_root = repo_root / ARCHIVE_DIRNAME
        team_root_path = repo_root / TEAM_DIRNAME
        if scope not in ARCHIVE_SCOPES:
            raise ScopeError(
                f"scope {scope!r} is outside the closed set {' / '.join(ARCHIVE_SCOPES)}")
    
        resolved_spec = _resolve_spec_dir(repo_root, spec_dir)
        if scope == "goal":
            return Scope("goal", repo_root, resolved_spec, goal_root, team_root_path)
        if scope == "teams":
            return Scope("teams", repo_root, resolved_spec, goal_root, team_root_path)
    
        # `auto` (C-11): ONE candidate layer or a named refusal. Never writes.
        candidates: list[str] = []
        if team_slug:
            if (goal_root / team_slug / DEFINITION_FILENAME).is_file():
                candidates.append("goal")
            if _team_layer_contains(team_root_path, team_slug):
                candidates.append("teams")
        else:
            if _has_any_definition(goal_root):
                candidates.append("goal")
            if _has_team(team_root_path, None):
                candidates.append("teams")
        if not candidates:
            subject = f"slug {team_slug!r}" if team_slug else "any definition"
>           raise ScopeError(
                f"not-found: no archive layer contains {subject} "
                f"(checked {ARCHIVE_DIRNAME} and {TEAM_DIRNAME})")
E           goal_utils_scope.ScopeError: not-found: no archive layer contains any definition (checked .specify/anchoring and .specify/teams)

            "at=%d line=%s stage=%s event=heartbeat\n" % (NOW, ws.line, ws.stage),
            encoding="utf-8")
